Theorem 217.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For convergence analysis pairs (A,B) in homotopy theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 206 and the invariant I(A,B) = 269 mod 1.

Proof: By the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_206(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 206. The extension on the ambient space provides the required map, and the obstruction class in H^269(S, F) is trivial. QED.

Theorem 217.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: In the homotopy framework, every structural decomposition morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 981. The resulting operator achieves bound O(455/n^4).

Proof: Consider the structural decomposition operator L on the homotopy space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 981 for all k. By the structural decomposition theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 455. QED.
